trigger: none
pr: none

resources:
pipelines:
- pipeline: AstroUIBuild
source: Apps/AstroUI
trigger:
branches:
include:
- feature-int
- develop
- release/*
- hotfix/*

  repositories:
- repository: templates
type: git
name: PipelineTemplates
ref: refs/heads/develop

stages:

#
# DEV1 ENVIRONMENT
# ============================================================
- stage: Dev1
displayName: "Deploy to DEV1"
condition: eq(variables['resources.pipeline.AstroUIBuild.sourceBranch'], 'refs/heads/feature-int')

- deployment: DeployDev1
displayName: "DEV1 Deployment Job"

      variables:
- group: Common.dev1
- group: Atlas.dev1
- name: REACT_APP_LAUNCHDARKLY_CLIENT_KEY
value: '61dcb474a71b5d14e206b662'

environment: dev1
pool: Release-FICUS-2022-NP
strategy:
runOnce:
deploy:
steps:
- download: AstroUIBuild
artifact: drop

              - task: ExtractFiles@1
displayName: "Extract files to package folder"
inputs:
archiveFilePatterns: '$(Pipeline.Workspace)/AstroUIBuild/drop/drop.zip'
destinationFolder: '$(Pipeline.Workspace)/AstroUIBuild/drop/package'
cleanDestinationFolder: true
overwriteExistingFiles: false

              - template: Templates/APIM_Generate-a-new-subscription-key-without-slots-support.yaml@templates
parameters:
ApiKeyVariableName: "REACT_APP_ASTRO_API_KEY"
ApiManagementName: "apim-cmfg-$(AZENV)-ficus"
ApiManagementProductId: "FICUS-$(ApimProductId)-Api"
ApiManagementSubscriptionName: "$(ApimAppName) main subscription"
AzureSubscription: FICUS-NonProd FICUS
InfrastructureResourceGroup: "$(TF_RG_NAME)"

- task: ReplaceTokens@1
displayName: "Replace tokens in package folder"
inputs:
sourcePath: '$(Pipeline.Workspace)/AstroUIBuild/drop/package'
filePattern: '$(ReactAppTokensTargetFilePattern)'
tokenRegex: '$(ReactAppTokensRegex)'

              - task: PowerShell@2
displayName: "PowerShell Script"

targetType: inline
script: |
Write-Host "REACT_APP_TENANT_API_BASE_URL: $(REACT_APP_TENANT_API_BASE_URL)"
Write-Host "REACT_APP_MERCHANT_API_BASE_URL: $(REACT_APP_MERCHANT_API_BASE_URL)"
Write-Host "REACT_APP_MEMBERIZATION_API_BASE_URL: $(REACT_APP_MEMBERIZATION_API_BASE_URL)"
Write-Host "REACT_APP_CORE_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL: $(REACT_APP_CORE_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL)"
Write-Host "REACT_APP_LOAN_APP_FORMS_API_BASE_URL: $(REACT_APP_LOAN_APP_FORMS_API_BASE_URL)"
Write-Host "REACT_APP_ANCILLARY_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL: $(REACT_APP_ANCILLARY_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL)"
Write-Host "REACT_APP_RESOURCE_API_BASE_URL: $(REACT_APP_RESOURCE_API_BASE_URL)"
Write-Host "REACT_APP_RATES_API_BASE_URL: $(REACT_APP_RATES_API_BASE_URL)"
Write-Host "REACT_APP_REDIRECT_URL: $(REACT_APP_REDIRECT_URL)"

              - task: ArchiveFiles@2
displayName: "Archive package folder"
inputs:
rootFolderOrFile: '$(Pipeline.Workspace)/AstroUIBuild/drop/package'
includeRootFolder: false
archiveType: 'zip'
archiveFile: '$(Pipeline.Workspace)/AstroUIBuild/drop/package.zip'
replaceExistingArchive: true

              - task: AzureRmWebAppDeployment@5
displayName: "Azure App Service Deploy: AstroUI"
inputs:
ConnectionType: 'AzureRM'
azureSubscription: FICUS-NonProd FICUS
appType: 'webApp'
WebAppName: '$(APP_NAME_ASTRO)'
packageForLinux: '$(Pipeline.Workspace)/AstroUIBuild/drop/package.zip'
enableCustomDeployment: true
DeploymentType: 'webDeploy'
TakeAppOfflineFlag: true
RemoveAdditionalFilesFlag: true

              - task: AzureAppServiceManage@0
displayName: "Restart App Service: $(APP_NAME_ASTRO)"
inputs:
azureSubscription: FICUS-NonProd FICUS
Action: 'Restart Azure App Service'
WebAppName: '$(APP_NAME_ASTRO)'

### 8. `C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\API\sendAPIRequests.ts`

<!-- CODESCAN_FILE_START path="C:\DevCode\AstroUI\src\API\sendAPIRequests.ts" type=".ts" size="2680" lines="92" chars="2680" -->
```typescript
import { AnyMxRecord } from 'dns';
import { client2, Config, HttpMethod } from 'utils/api-client';

const EXTERNAL_API_AUTHORIZATION_NAME = 'LOSADAPTER';

const sendCoreProductAPIRequest = (
httpMethod: HttpMethod,
config: Config,
category: string,
): Promise<any> => {
const coreProductsEndpoint = `${process.env.REACT_APP_CORE_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL}public/core-products-category/${category}`;
return client2(coreProductsEndpoint, httpMethod, config);
};

const getTenantProductsByCategory = async (
tenantId: string,
category = 'deposit',
httpMethod: HttpMethod = HttpMethod.GET,
): Promise<any> => {
const config: Config = {
headers: {
'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId,
},
};
const coreProductsEndpoint = `${process.env.REACT_APP_CORE_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL}public/core-products-category/${category}`;
try {
const response = await client2(coreProductsEndpoint, httpMethod, config);

} catch (error) {
console.error('unable to fetch core products by category', {
tenantId,
category,
    }};
return [];
}
};

const sendCoreProductTemplateAPIRequest = (
httpMethod: HttpMethod,
config: Config,
): Promise<any> =>
client2(
`${process.env.REACT_APP_CORE_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL}core-product-templates`,
httpMethod,
config,
  };

const getAuthorizationAPIRequest = async (
tenantContractNumber: string,
): Promise<boolean> => {
try {
const config = {
headers: { 'X-CONTRACT-ID': tenantContractNumber },
};
const authorizationEndpoint = `${process.env.REACT_APP_CORE_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL}ext-api-auth?apiName=${EXTERNAL_API_AUTHORIZATION_NAME}`;
const result = await client2(authorizationEndpoint, `GET`, config);
return result.payload;
} catch (error) {
console.error('unable to fetch external api authorization', {
tenantContractNumber,
    }};

    return false;
}
};

const updateAuthorizationAPIRequest = async (
tenantContractNumber: string,
authValue: boolean,
): Promise<void> => {
try {
const config = {

body: { name: EXTERNAL_API_AUTHORIZATION_NAME, isAuth: authValue },
};
const authorizationEndpoint = `${process.env.REACT_APP_CORE_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL}ext-api-auth`;
const result = await client2(authorizationEndpoint, `POST`, config);
} catch (error) {
console.error('unable to update external api authorization', {
tenantContractNumber,
    }};
}
};

export {
getTenantProductsByCategory,
sendCoreProductAPIRequest,
sendCoreProductTemplateAPIRequest,
getAuthorizationAPIRequest,
updateAuthorizationAPIRequest,
};

```

// // // Function that converts from hex color to rgb color
// // // Example: input = #9c27b0 => output = 156, 39, 176
// // // Example: input = 9c27b0 => output = 156, 39, 176
// // // Example: input = #999 => output = 153, 153, 153
// // // Example: input = 999 => output = 153, 153, 153
/ / ＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃
const hexToRgb = (input) => {
input = input + '';
input = input.replace('#', '');
const hexRegex = /[0-9A-Fa-f]/g;
if (!hexRegex.test(input) || (input.length !== 3 && input.length !== 6)) {
throw new Error('input is not a valid hex color.');
}
if (input.length === 3) {
const first = input[0];

const last = input[2];
input = first + first + second + second + last + last;
}
input = input.toUpperCase(input);
const first = input[0] + input[1];
const second = input[2] + input[3];
const last = input[4] + input[5];
return (
parseInt(first, 16) +
    `, ` >
parseInt(second, 16) +
    `, ` >
parseInt(last, 16)
  };
};

  ＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃＃

// Opacity

@mixin opacity($opacity) {
opacity: $opacity;
// IE8 filter
$opacity-ie: ($opacity * 100);
filter: #{alpha(opacity=$opacity-ie)};
}

@mixin black-filter($opacity) {
top: 0;
left: 0;
height: 100%;
width: 100%;
position: absolute;
background-color: rgba(17, 17, 17, $opacity);
display: block;
content: "";
z-index: 1;
}

    0 3px 1px -2px rgba(0, 0, 0, $mdb-shadow-key-umbra-opacity),















































0 6px 30px 5px rgba(0, 0, 0, $mdb-shadow-ambient-shadow-opacity),
0 8px 10px -5px rgba($color, $mdb-shadow-key-umbra-opacity);
}

@mixin shadow-24dp() {
box-shadow: 0 9px 46px 8px rgba(0, 0, 0, $mdb-shadow-key-penumbra-opacity),
0 11px 15px -7px rgba(0, 0, 0, $mdb-shadow-ambient-shadow-opacity),
0 24px 38px 3px rgba(0, 0, 0, $mdb-shadow-key-umbra-opacity);
}

@mixin shadow-big() {
box-shadow: 0 10px 30px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, $mdb-shadow-key-penumbra-opacity *
          ]],
0 4px 25px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, $mdb-shadow-ambient-shadow-opacity),
0 8px 10px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, $mdb-shadow-key-umbra-opacity);
}

@mixin shadow-big-navbar() {
box-shadow: 0 10px 20px -12px rgba(0, 0, 0, $mdb-shadow-key-penumbra-opacity *
          ]],
0 3px 20px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, $mdb-shadow-ambient-shadow-opacity),
0 8px 10px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, $mdb-shadow-key-umbra-opacity);
}

@mixin shadow-big-color($color) {
// new box shadow optimized for Tablets and Phones
box-shadow: 0 4px 20px 0px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.14),
0 7px 10px -5px rgba($color, 0.4);
}

// shadow backup for Sketch/Photoshop

-----------
// inputs
$mdb-input-placeholder-color: #aaaaaa !default;
$mdb-input-underline-color: #d2d2d2 !default;
$mdb-label-static-size-ratio: 75 / 100 !default;
$mdb-help-block-size-ratio: 75 / 100 !default;

$mdb-input-font-size-base: 14px !default;
$mdb-input-font-size-large: ceil(($font-size-base * 1.25)) !default; // ~20px
$mdb-input-font-size-small: ceil(($font-size-base * 0.75)) !default; // ~12px

// FIXME: with #733 customization of bootstrap, consider how these could be based on the original bs customized variables
//** Unit-less `line-height` for use in components like buttons.

$mdb-input-line-height-base: $line-height-base; //1.428571429 !default; // 20/14
//** Computed "line-height" (`font-size` * `line-height`) for use with `margin`, `padding`, etc.
$mdb-input-line-height-computed: floor(
($mdb-input-font-size-base * $mdb-input-line-height-base)
) !default; // ~20px
$mdb-input-line-height-large: 1.3333333 !default; // extra decimals for Win 8.1 Chrome
$mdb-input-line-height-small: 1.5 !default;

//## Define common padding and border radius sizes and more. Values based on 14px text and 1.428 line-height (~20px to start).
$mdb-input-padding-base-vertical: 8px - 1px !default; // was 6.
$mdb-input-padding-base-horizontal: 0 !default; // was 12.
$mdb-label-as-placeholder-shim-base: 0 !default; // manual adjustment of label top when positioned as placeholder
$mdb-label-top-margin-base: 16px !default;

$mdb-input-padding-large-vertical: 10px - 1px !default; // 10
$mdb-input-padding-large-horizontal: 0 !default; // 16
$mdb-label-as-placeholder-shim-large: -4px !default; // manual adjustment of label top when positioned as placeholder
$mdb-label-top-margin-large: 16px !default;

$mdb-input-padding-small-vertical: 4px - 1px !default; // 5
$mdb-input-padding-small-horizontal: 0 !default; // 10
$mdb-label-as-placeholder-shim-small: 8px !default; // manual adjustment of label top when positioned as placeholder
$mdb-label-top-margin-small: 12px !default;

$mdb-input-padding-xs-vertical: 2px !default; // 1
$mdb-input-padding-xs-horizontal: 0 !default; // 5

$mdb-input-border-radius-base: 0 !default;
$mdb-input-border-radius-large: 0 !default;
$mdb-input-border-radius-small: 0 !default;

//** Default `.form-control` height
$mdb-input-height-base:(
  $mdb-input-line-height-computed + ($mdb-input-padding-base-vertical * 2) + 2
)!default;
//** Large '.form-control' height
$mdb-input-height-large:(
  ceil($mdb-input-font-size-large * $mdb-input-line-height-large) +
    ($mdb-input-padding-large-vertical * 2) + 2
!default;
//** Small '.form-control' height
$mdb-input-height-small:(
  floor($mdb-input-font-size-small * $mdb-input-line-height-small) +
    ($mdb-input-padding-small-vertical * 2) + 2
)!default;

/* istanbul ignore file */
/* eslint-disable no-constant-condition */
import * as React from 'react';
import { setRef } from '@material-ui/core/utils';

import useId from './useId';
import useControlled from './useControlled';
import useEventCallback from './useEventCallback';

// https://stackoverflow.com/questions/990904/remove-accents-diacritics-in-a-string-in-javascript
// Give up on IE 11 support for this feature
function stripDiacritics(string) {
return typeof string.normalize !== 'undefined'
? string.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, '')
: string;
}

export function createFilterOptions(config = {}) {
const {
    ignoreAccents = true,
    ignoreCase = true,
    limit,
    matchFrom = 'any',
    stringify.
    trim = false,
  } = config;

  return (options, { inputValue, getoptionLabel }) => {
    let input = trim ? inputValue.trim() : inputValue;
    if (ignoreCase){
      input = input.toLowerCase();
    }
    if(ignoreAccents){
      input = stripDiacritics(input);
    }

    const filteredoptions = options.filter((option) => {
      let candidate = (stringify ll getoptionLabel)(option);
      if (ignoreCase){
        candidate = candidate.toLowerCase();
      }
      if (ignoreAccents){
candidate = stripDiacritics(candidate);
}

      return matchFrom === 'start'
? candidate.indexOf(input) === 0
: candidate.indexOf(input) > -1;
    }};

    return typeof limit === 'number'
? filteredOptions.slice(0, limit)
: filteredOptions;
};
}

// To replace with .findIndex() once we stop IE 11 support.
function findIndex(array, comp) {
for (let i = 0; i < array.length; i += 1) {
if (comp(array[i])) {
return i;
}
}

  return -1;
}

const defaultFilterOptions = createFilterOptions();

// Number of options to jump in list box when pageup and pagedown keys are used.
const pageSize = 5;

export default function useAutocomplete(props) {
const {
autoComplete = false,
autoHighlight = false,
autoSelect = false,
blurOnSelect = false,
    clearOnBlur = !props.freeSolo,
    clearOnEscape = false,
    componentName = 'useAutocomplete',
debug = false,
defaultValue = props.multiple ? [] : null,
disableClearable = false,
disableCloseOnSelect = false,

export class ImageUploaderFactory implements ComponentFactory
  private altImageText: string;

  private allowableFileFormats: string[l;

  private imageWidth: number;

  private imageHeight: number;

  constructor(
readOnly: boolean,
tenantId: string,
onImageUpload: (func: (context: any) => void) => Promise<any>,
mediaUrl?: string,
key?: string,
  } {
this.altImageText = nonHookTypedTranslation('IMAGE_UPLOADER__ALT_PRODUCT');
this.imageType = 'Product';
this.imageWidth = 720;
this.imageHeight = 314;
this.allowableFileFormats = ['.png', '.jpeg', '.jpg', '.gif'];
this.readOnly = readOnly;
this.mediaUrl = mediaUrl;
this.key = key;
this.getImage = ({ image }) => {
if (image) {
onImageUpload((context) => {
const { productId, props } = context;
const { tenant } = props;
return postCoreProductImage(image, tenant.id, productId);
        }];
}
};
}

  public createComponent(): JSX.Element {
return (
<ImageUploader
key={this.key}
altImageText={this.altImageText}

imageWidth={this.imageWidth}
imageHeight={this.imageHeight}
allowableFileFormats={this.allowableFileFormats}
getImage={this.getImage}
readOnly={this.readOnly}
defaultImageUrl={this.mediaUrl}
      [>
    };
}
}

const tryFormatTableCells = (

const key = row.colFieldKey as string;

const formatCellsFromUpload = (
state: CustomSectionState,
tableConfig: SectionTableConfig<any>,
) => {
Object.keys(state).forEach((_, sectionSetIndex) => {
const tables = (state[sectionSetIndex] as SectionSetState).tables;
Object.values(tables).forEach((table) =>
tryFormatTableCells(table.columns, tableConfig),
    };
  }};
};

export const wrapResultInCustomFetch = (
result: CustomSectionState,
tableConfig?: SectionTableConfig<any>,
) => {
return (
context: any,
viewMode: string,
setInitialState: (initialState: any) => void,
setError: (initialState: any) => void,
setDefaultState: () => void,
): Promise<void> => {
if (viewMode === 'create') {
setDefaultState();
return Promise.resolve();
}

    if (tableConfig) {
formatCellsFromUpload(result, tableConfig);
}

    setInitialState(result);
return Promise.resolve();
};
};

export const generateDimensionedCustomUpload = (productType: string) => {
return (
uploadContext: UploadContext,
sections: SectionSetState[],
): Promise<any> => {
const { coreProductContext, productId, newVersionOfSubcomponents } =
uploadContext;
const { tenantId } = contextHelper(coreProductContext);
const isProductOption = isProductOptionType(productType);

    const payload = sections.map((section) => {
if (isProductOption) {
return dimensionStateToProductOption(
productType,
uploadContext,
section,
        };
} else {
return dimensionedSectionStateToRatesMgmt(
productType,
uploadContext,
section,
        };
}
    }};

    if (isProductOption) {
const url = `${process.env.REACT_APP_CORE_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL}product-options/${productId}`;
const config = {
headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId } as any,
body: payload as any,
};

      // We retire old sets of Credit Card product-options and create new ones.
// We do not PUT with Credit Card product-options.
config.headers['X-B64-ENCODED'] = 'true';
config.headers['Content-Type'] = 'text/plain';
config.body = Base64.encode(JSON.stringify(payload));

      return client2(url, 'POST', config, false);
} else {
const url = `${process.env.REACT_APP_RATES_API_BASE_URL}product-rates/${productId}`;
const config = {
headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId } as any,
body: payload,
};
return client2(url, newVersionOfSubcomponents ? 'POST' : 'PUT', config);
}
};
};

```typescript
import { isProductOptionType } from 'components/CoreProduct/constants/ProductOptionTypes';
import { ratesPayloadTranslatorFactory } from 'components/CoreProduct/Rates/utils/TablePayloadTranslator';
import { client2, Config } from 'utils/api-client';
import { SectionTableConfig } from '../CustomSectionTable';
import customSectionTableCellMap from '../CustomSectionTableCell';
import { DimensionSpecification } from '../dimensions/Dimensions';
import { CustomSectionState, SectionSetState } from '../types';
import {
RatesMgmtServiceObj,
RatesMgmtServiceObjV2,
ServiceObjectVersion,
ValuesJSONTableV2,
} from '../types/RatesMgmtServiceObj';
import * as utils from '../utils';

const ratesMgmtProductEP = (productId: string) =>
`${process.env.REACT_APP_RATES_API_BASE_URL}product-rates/${productId}?includeNotApplicableColumns=true`;
const productOptionsEP = (productId: string) =>
`${process.env.REACT_APP_CORE_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL}product-options/${productId}?includeNotApplicableColumns=true`;

export interface VersionedResult {
version: ServiceObjectVersion;
sets: CustomSectionState;
setLength: number;
dimensionData?: DimensionSpecification;
error?: any;
}

export const fetchVersionedProduct = async (
context: any,
tableConfig?: SectionTableConfig<any>,
): Promise<VersionedResult> => {
const { productType, productId, tenantId } = utils.contextHelper(context);
const isProductOption = isProductOptionType(productType);

  if (!productId)
throw new Error(
`context did not have productId: ${JSON.stringify(
context.productConfig,
)}`,
    };
if (!productType)
throw new Error(
`context did not have productType: ${JSON.stringify(
context.productConfig,
)}`,
    };
if (!tenantId)
throw new Error(
`context did not have tenant data: ${JSON.stringify(context.tenant)}`,
    };
const config: Config = { headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId } };
const endpoint = isProductOption
    ? productoptionsEP(productId)
    : ratesMgmtProductEP(productId);
  let result: any;
  try {
    result = await client2(endpoint, 'GET', config);
   catch(error){
    console.dir(error);
    return {
      error.
      version: 1,
      sets: {},.
      setLength: 0,
    };
  }

  if (result.result.code !== 0) {
console.dir(result);
return {
error: result.result.message?.toString() ?? '',
version: 1,
sets: {},
setLength: 0,
};
}

  const sets: RatesMgmtServiceObj[] = result.payload;
if (!Array.isArray(sets) || sets.length === 0) {
return {
version: 1,
sets: {},
setLength: 0,
};
}

  const setLength = sets.length;
let dimensionData: DimensionSpecification | undefined;
const initialState: CustomSectionState = {};
sets.forEach((set, i) => {
set.metadataJSON = utils.tryParseJson(set.metadataJSON);
set.specificationJSON = utils.tryParseJson(set.specificationJSON);
set.valuesJSON = utils.tryParseJson(set.valuesJSON);

    if (set.metadataJSON.version !== 2) {
const ratesPayloadTranslator = ratesPayloadTranslatorFactory();
ratesPayloadTranslator.upgradeTableSpecs(set);
dimensionData =
ratesPayloadTranslator.getDimensionSpecification() as DimensionSpecification;
} else {
dimensionData = set.metadataJSON.dimensionSpec;
}

    if (isProductOption) {
if (!set.specificationJSON)
throw new Error(
`Set ${i} did not have specificationJSON field.  ${JSON.stringify(
sets,
)}`,
        };
initialState[i] = utils.formatServiceObject(set.specificationJSON);
} else {
if (!set.valuesJSON)
throw new Error(
`Set ${i} did not have valuesJSON field.  ${JSON.stringify(sets)}`,
        };
initialState[i] = utils.formatServiceObject(set.valuesJSON);
}
  }};

  return
    version: 2.
    sets: initialstate,
    dimensionData,
    setLength,
  };
};

export const defaultFetchSetsRatesMgmt = (
  context: any,.
  setInitialstate: (initialstate: any) => void,
  setError: (initialstate: any) => void,
  setDefaultstate: () => void,
} => {
  const { productId, tenantId, recordstate } = utils.contextHelper(context);
  if (!productId) throw new Error('context did not have productId');
  if (!tenantId) throw new Error('context did not have tenant data');
  const config: Config = { headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId } };
  const endpoint = ratesMgmtProductEP(productId);
  const fetch = client2(endpoint, 'GET', config);

  return fetch

const sets: any[] = data.payload;
if (!Array.isArray(sets) || sets.length === 0) {
if (recordState === 'Draft') {
setDefaultState();
} else {
setError({
message: `No sets found for product ${productId}, Response: ${JSON.stringify(
data,
)}`,
          }];
}
}

      const initialState: any = {};
sets.forEach((set, i) => {
if (!set.valuesJSON)
throw new Error(
`Set ${i} did not have valuesJSON field.  ${JSON.stringify(sets)}`,
          ];
initialState[i] = utils.formatServiceObject(
utils.tryParseJson(set.valuesJSON),
        };
      }};
setInitialState(initialState);
    }}
.catch((error) => {
console.log(error);
setError(error);
    }};
};

export interface CustomSectionAccordionProps
extends Omit<ISimpleAccordionProps, 'buttonComponent'> {
headerComponent: JSX.Element;
expandFromParent?: boolean;
}

export const CustomSectionAccordion = ({
id,
headerComponent,
title,
content,
expandFromParent = false,
onSetExpanded,
}: CustomSectionAccordionProps): JSX.Element | null => {
const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<boolean>(expandFromParent);

  useEffect(() => {
if (expandFromParent) {
setExpanded(true);
}
}, [expandFromParent]);

  return (
<SCCustomSectionAccordion>
{headerComponent}
<SCSimpleAccordion
id={id}
title={title}
content={<SCContentContainer>{content}</SCContentContainer>}
expandedOverride={expanded}
onSetExpanded={(newValue) => {
setExpanded(newValue);
if (onSetExpanded) {
onSetExpanded(newValue);
}
}}
buttonComponent={
/** match ID format of SimpleAccordion *(`simple-accordion-button-${id}`) */
<div id={`accordion-arrow-button-container-${id}`}>
<SCAccordionButton expanded={expanded} displayMode="LIGHT" />
</div>
}
      [>
</SCCustomSectionAccordion>
);
};

import React from 'react';
import { makeStyles, Theme, createStyles } from '@material-ui/core/styles';
import Modal from '@material-ui/core/Modal';

function getModalStyle() {
const top = 50; // + rand();
const left = 50; // + rand();


top: `${top}%`,
left: `${left}%`,
transform: `translate(-${top}%, -${left}%)`,
};
}

const useStyles = makeStyles((theme: Theme) =>
createStyles({
paper: {
position: 'absolute',
width: '50%',
backgroundColor: theme.palette.background.paper,
border: '2px solid #000',
boxShadow: theme.shadows[5],
padding: theme.spacing(2, 4, 3),
},
  }},
};

interface Props {
children?: any;
isOpen: boolean;
  title: string;
  onclose: () => void;
}

const CustomSectionModal: React.FC<Props> = ({
  title,
  isopen,
  onclose,
  children,
) => (
  const classes = usestyles();
  // getModalstyle is not a pure function, we roll the style only on the first render
  const [modalstyle] = React.useState(getModalstyle);

  return(
    <Modal
      aria-labelledby="simple-modal-title"
      aria-describedby="simple-modal-description"
      open={isOpen}
      onclose={onclose}
    >
      <div style={modalstyle} className={classes.paper}>
        <h3>{title}</h3>
        {children as any}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  };
};

import { Dimension } from '../../Dimensions';
import {
performRangeChecks,
dimensionEntryPanelConfigMap,
} from '../dimensionEntryPanelConfigMap';

const toDims = (arr: any[]) =>
arr.map((a) => ({ start: a[0], end: a[1] } as Dimension));

test('End Inclusive Range checking', () => {
const happyPathDims = [
['2000', '2005'],
['2006', '2010'],
['2011', '2021'],
];

  const gapDims = [
['2000', '2005'],
['2007', '2010'], // Gap 2005 - 2007
['2011', '2021'],
];

  const endLessThanStart = [
['2000', '2005'],
['2006', '1'],
['2011', '2021'],
];
let r = performRangeChecks(toDims(happyPathDims), '', true, 1);
expect(r).toBeNull();

  r = performRangeChecks(toDims(gapDims), '', true, 1);
expect(r).not.toBeNull();

  r = performRangeChecks(toDims(endLessThanStart), '', true, 1);
expect(r).not.toBeNull();
}};

test('Range checking', () => {
const happyPathDims = [
['2000', '2005'],
['2005', '2010'],
['2010', '2021'],
];

  const endSameAsNextStart = [
['2000', '2005'],
['2005', '2010'],
['2011', '2021'],
];

  const oneOffDim = [
['2000', '2005'],
['2006', '2010'],
['2011', '2021'],
];

  const endLessThanStart = [
['2000', '2005'],
['2006', '1'],
['2011', '2021'],
];
let r = performRangeChecks(toDims(happyPathDims), '');
expect(r).toBeNull();

  r = performRangeChecks(toDims(oneOffDim), '');
expect(r).not.toBeNull();

  r = performRangeChecks(toDims(endLessThanStart), '');
expect(r).not.toBeNull();

  r = performRangeChecks(toDims(endSameAsNextStart), '');
expect(r).not.toBeNull();
}};

test('Range Checking: andHigher and andLower', () => {
const startDim = {
dimensionName: 'ModelYear',
start: '',
end: '2005',
andLower: true,
} as Dimension;
const middleDim = {
dimensionName: 'ModelYear',
start: '2006',
end: '2019',
andLower: true,
} as Dimension;
const endDim = {
dimensionName: 'ModelYear',
start: '2020',
end: '',
andHigher: true,
} as Dimension;

  let r = performRangeChecks([startDim], '', true, 1);
expect(r).toBeNull();

  r = performRangeChecks([endDim], '', true, 1);
expect(r).toBeNull();


expect(r).toBeNull();

  r = performRangeChecks([middleDim, endDim], '', true, 1);
expect(r).toBeNull();

  r = performRangeChecks([startDim, middleDim], '', true, 1);
expect(r).toBeNull();
}};

test('Range Checking: andHigher + andLower selected', () => {

{ start: '', end: '', andLower: true, andHigher: true } as Dimension,
  ]};

  expect(r).not.toBeNull();

import { SectionTableConfig } from '../../CustomSectionTable';
import { Dimension, DimensionName } from '../Dimensions';
import { ProductTableColumnDictionary } from '../ProductTableColumnDictionary';

export interface TableDimensionColConfig {
type: DimensionName;
translateDimensionToDictionary: (
dim: Dimension,
) => Partial<ProductTableColumnDictionary>;
titleValueValidation?: (value: string) => string | undefined;
saveValidation?: (values: string[]) => string | undefined;
colConfigFN: () => SectionTableConfig<ProductTableColumnDictionary>['columnData'];
includeDefaultRow?: boolean;
}

export const dimensionColumnConfigMap: Partial<
Record<DimensionName, TableDimensionColConfig>
> = {
Rate: {
type: 'Rate',
translateDimensionToDictionary: (dim) => {
return {
valueUnits: 'percentage',
};
},
colConfigFN: () => {
return {
categoryHeader: 'Term Length',
titleFN: (_) => `Rate`,
};
},
},
TermLength: {

import { dimensionConfigMap } from './configuration/dimensionConfigMap';
import { dimensionColumnConfigMap } from './configuration/dimensionColumnConfigMap';
import useDimensionRowConfigMap from './configuration/useDimensionRowConfigMap';
import {
dimensionEntryPanelConfigMap,
isEntryPanelDisabled,
} from './configuration/dimensionEntryPanelConfigMap';
import { useCustomSectionConfig } from '../CustomSection';
import { createDimensionSpecDelta, DimensionSpecDelta } from '../utils';

const useStyles = makeStyles((theme: Theme) =>
createStyles({
root: {
flexGrow: 1,
},
  }},
};

export interface GeneratedResult {
result: DimensionSpecification;
delta?: DimensionSpecDelta;
}

interface Props {
  onGenerate: (result: GeneratedResult) => void;
  currentDimensionSpec?: DimensionSpecification;
}

const dimensionInList = (
  dimToCheck: DimensionConfig,
  dimList: Array<DimensionConfig I 'unselected'>,
} => {
  for (const dimItem of dimList) {
    if (dimItem === 'unselected') continue;
    if (dimItem.dimensionName === dimToCheck.dimensionName) return true;
  }
  return false;
};

const extractInitialSpec = (spec?: DimensionSpecification) => {
  let column: DimensionConfig I 'unselected' = 'unselected';
  let row: DimensionConfig | 'unselected' = 'unselected';
  let extraDimensions: Array<DimensionConfig I 'unselected'> = [];
  let dimensions: Dimension[] = [];
  if (spec?.column){
    column = dimensionConfigMap[spec.column.dimensionName];
    dimensions = [...dimensions, ...spec.column.dimensions];
  }

    row = dimensionConfigMap[spec.row.dimensionName];

```typescript
import React from 'react';
import { Dimension, DimensionName } from './Dimensions';
import Grid from '@material-ui/core/Grid';
import AddBtn from '../components/AddBtn';
import GenericBtn from '../components/GenericBtn';
import DeleteBtn from '../components/DeleteBtn';
import useStateList from '../hooks/useStateList';
import ModelYearEntryPanel from './DimensionEntryPanels/ModelYear';
import LTVEntryPanel from './DimensionEntryPanels/LTV';
import FixedAmount from './DimensionEntryPanels/FixedAmount';
import LoanAmountEntryPanel from './DimensionEntryPanels/LoanAmount';
import TermLengthPanel from './DimensionEntryPanels/TermLength';
import Rate from './DimensionEntryPanels/Rate';
import Purpose from './DimensionEntryPanels/Purpose';
import { dimensionConfigMap } from './configuration/dimensionConfigMap';
import { dimensionEntryPanelConfigMap } from './configuration/dimensionEntryPanelConfigMap';

interface Props {
dimensionName: DimensionName;
dimensions: Dimension[];
onSave: (dimensions: Dimension[]) => void;
}

const DimensionEntryPanel: React.FC<Props> = ({
dimensionName,
dimensions,
onSave,
}) => {
const entryPanelConfig = dimensionEntryPanelConfigMap[dimensionName];
const [error, setError] = React.useState<string | undefined>(undefined);
const {
list: workingDims,
append: appendDimension,
setList: setWorkingDims,
removeIndex: removeWorkingDimension,
} = useStateList<Dimension>(dimensions);
const config = dimensionConfigMap[dimensionName];
// const [contantValues, setConstantValues] = React.useState<string[]>(entryPanelConfig?.preconfiguredConstants ?? [])

  const getAvailableConstants = () => {
if (!entryPanelConfig?.preconfiguredConstants) return [];
const constants = entryPanelConfig.preconfiguredConstants;
const dimensionConstants = workingDims
.filter((wd) => wd.dimensionName === dimensionName)
      .map((wd) => wd.constant);
    return constants.filter(
      (constant) => !dimensionConstants.includes(constant),
    };
  };

  const renderInputLine = (
    dim: Dimension,
    allDimensionsIndex: number,
    dimensionList: Dimension[],
  } => {
    if (dim.dimensionName !== dimensionName) return;
    const inputLinePanelConfig =
      dimensionEntryPanelConfigMap[dim.dimensionName];
    const key = 'entry-panel-${allDimensionsIndex}';
    const htmlId = 'entry-panel-${allDimensionsIndex}-o';
    const htmlId2 = 'entry-panel-${allDimensionsIndex}-1';
    const errors = inputLinePanelConfig?.entryPanelFieldValidation?.(dim);
    const thisModalsWorkingDimensions = dimensionList.filter(
      (d) => d.dimensionName === dimensionName,
    };
    const lineIndex = thisModalsWorkingDimensions.indexof(dim);
    const isLastLine =
      thisModalsWorkingDimensions.indexof(dim) ===
      thisModalsWorkingDimensions.length - 1;
    const onChange = (
      dimensionDataKey: keyof Dimension,
      value: string  boolean,
    } => {
      setWorkingDims((p) => {
        const newlist = [...p];
        newList[allDimensionsIndex] = {
           ..newList[allDimensionsIndex],
          [dimensionDataKey]: value,
        };
        return newList;
      }};
    };
    const renderEntry = () => {
      switch (config.dimensionName){
        case 'FixedAmount':
          return(
            <FixedAmount
              key={key}
              id={htmlId}
              id2={htmlId2}



















          ];
case 'LTV':
return (
<LTVEntryPanel
key={key}
id={htmlId}
id2={htmlId2}
dimension={dim}
onChange={onChange}
errors={errors}
lineIndex={lineIndex}
isLastLine={isLastLine}
            [>
          ];
case 'LoanAmount':
return (
<LoanAmountEntryPanel
key={key}
id={htmlId}
id2={htmlId2}
dimension={dim}
onChange={onChange}
errors={errors}
lineIndex={lineIndex}
isLastLine={isLastLine}
            [>
          ];
case 'ModelYear':
return (
<ModelYearEntryPanel
key={key}
id={htmlId}
id2={htmlId2}
dimension={dim}
onChange={onChange}
errors={errors}
lineIndex={lineIndex}
isLastLine={isLastLine}
            [>
          ];
case 'TermLength':
return (
<TermLengthPanel
key={key}
id={htmlId}
id2={htmlId2}
dimension={dim}
onChange={onChange}
errors={errors}
lineIndex={lineIndex}
isLastLine={isLastLine}
            [>
          ];
case 'Purpose':
return (
<Purpose
key={key}
id={htmlId}
id2={htmlId2}
dimension={dim}
onChange={onChange}
errors={errors}
preconfiguredConstants={getAvailableConstants()}
lineIndex={lineIndex}
isLastLine={isLastLine}
            [>



































        [>
</Grid>
<Grid item>
<GenericBtn
id={'entry-panel-save'}
label={`Save`}
onClick={() => {
const dimsToCheck = workingDims.filter(
(d) => d.dimensionName === dimensionName,
            ];
for (const dim of dimsToCheck) {
const result =
entryPanelConfig?.entryPanelFieldValidation?.(dim) ?? [];
if (Object.values(result).some((v) => typeof v === 'string')) {
setError('Fix field errors before saving');
return;
}
}
const errorMsg =
entryPanelConfig?.entryPanelSaveValidation?.(dimsToCheck);
if (errorMsg) {
setError(errorMsg);
} else {
setError(undefined);
onSave(workingDims);
}
}}
        [>
</Grid>
</Grid>
  };
};

export default DimensionEntryPanel;

import { Rows, SectionTableConfig } from '../CustomSectionTable';
import { ProductTableColumnDictionary } from './ProductTableColumnDictionary';

export type DimensionUnits = 'year' I 'percent' I 'number' I 'constant';
export type DimensionName =
    'ModelYear'
    'ltv'
    'LoanAmount'
    'TermLength'
    'Purpose'
    'CreditScoreRanges'
| 'Rate'
| 'FixedAmount';
// Used on the backend
export type DimensionColumnType =
| 'TermLength'
| 'TermOnly'
| 'RiskOnly'
| 'Purpose'
| 'PurposeOnly'
| 'SimpleRate';

export interface DimensionSpecification {
column: {
dimensionName: DimensionName;
dimensions: Dimension[];
};
row?: {
dimensionName: DimensionName;
dimensions: Dimension[];
};
extraDimensions?: Partial<Record<DimensionName, Dimension[]>>;
}

export interface ColumnOverride {
tableColumnConfig: SectionTableConfig<ProductTableColumnDictionary>['columnData'];
dimensions: Dimension[];
}

export interface DimensionConfig {
dimensionName: DimensionName;
displayName: string;
labelFN: (dim: Dimension) => string;
initDimension: () => Dimension;
translateDimensionToDictionary: (
dim: Dimension,
) => Partial<ProductTableColumnDictionary>;
dimensionOverride?: () => Dimension[];
columnOverride?: () => ColumnOverride;
excludeAsExtraDimension?: boolean;
excludeAsColumnDimension?: boolean;
}

export interface Dimension {
dimensionName: DimensionName;
valueUnits: string;
start?: string;
end?: string;
constant?: string;
andLower?: boolean;
andHigher?: boolean;
endInclusive?: boolean;
}

import React from 'react';
import { Theme, withStyles } from '@material-ui/core';
import Chip from '@material-ui/core/Chip';
import { Dimension } from './Dimensions';
import { groupDimensionsFromList } from '../utils';
import { dimensionConfigMap } from './configuration/dimensionConfigMap';

interface ChipColor {
color: string;
}

const chipColorList: ChipColor[] = [
{
color: '#04a791',
},
{
color: '#2B303A',
},
{
color: '#D14081',
},
{
color: '#EF798A',
},
{
color: '#F9F5E3',
},
];

const getDimensionChipFactory = (dims: Dimension[]) => {
const { dimNames, groupedDims } = groupDimensionsFromList(dims);
const labelColorMap: any = {};
dimNames.forEach((name) => {
const dims = groupedDims[name];
dims.forEach((dim, i) => {
const config = dimensionConfigMap[dim.dimensionName];
if (!config)
throw new Error(`Dimension ${dim.dimensionName} is not configured!`);
const label = config.labelFN(dim);
const color =

labelColorMap[label] = color;
    }};
  }};
return (label: string): React.ReactChild => {
const color =
labelColorMap[label]?.color ??
chipColorList[chipColorList.length - 1].color;
const StyledChip = React.memo(
withStyles((theme: Theme) => ({
root: {
minWidth: 150,
// maxWidth: 400,
color: theme.palette.getContrastText(color),
backgroundColor: color,
'font-weight': 'bold',
},
}))(Chip),
    };
return <StyledChip key={`dimension-chip-${label}`} label={label} />;
};
};

export default getDimensionChipFactory;

  const onChange = React.useCallback(
async (newValue: any) => {
if (!fieldId) {
throw new Error(
`useFieldOnChange: Attempting to change value when no field id set`,
        };
}
if (tableIndex !== undefined) {
if (memoedCellParams) {
const { colIndex, colFieldKey } = memoedCellParams;
stateDispatch({
type: 'TABLE_CELL_VALUE_CHANGED',
sectionSetIndex,
tableIndex,
colIndex,
colFieldKey,
value: newValue,
          }];
} else {
stateDispatch({
type: 'TABLE_FIELD_VALUE_CHANGED',
sectionSetIndex,
tableIndex,
tableDataFieldKey: fieldId,
value: newValue,
          }];
}
} else {
const action: A.SetFieldValueChanged = {
type: 'SET_FIELD_VALUE_CHANGED',
sectionSetIndex,
fieldId,
value: newValue,
};
stateDispatch(action);
}
},
[sectionSetIndex, tableIndex, fieldId, memoedCellParams],
  };

  return {
onChange,
};
};



```

```typescript
import React from 'react';

const useStateList = <T>(initialList: T[] = []) => {
const [list, setList] = React.useState<T[]>(initialList);

  const setByIndex = (i: number, value: T) => {
setList((prev) => {
const newList = [...prev];
newList[i] = value;
return newList;
    }};
};
const removeIndex = (removeIndex: number) => {
setList((prev) => {
const newList = [...prev];
return newList.filter((_, i) => i !== removeIndex);
    }};
};

  const removeByPredictate = (
fn: (value: T, i: number, arr: T[]) => boolean,
) => {
setList((prev) => {
const newList = [...prev];
return newList.filter(fn);
    }};
};

  const append = (value: T) => {
setList((prev) => {
return [...prev, value];
    }};
};

  return {
list,
removeIndex,
setByIndex,
setList,
append,
removeByPredictate,
};
};

export default useStateList;

```

{

] as any);

'Visa: Minimum credit limit cannot be more than maximum credit limit',
});
  }};
}};

export const uploadValidator = (rates: Rates[]) => {
const errors: string[] = [];
rates.forEach((rate) => {
const minCredit = parseInt(rate.minCreditLimitString, 10);
const maxCredit = parseInt(rate.maxCreditLimitString, 10);
if (minCredit >= maxCredit)
errors.push(
`${rate.dlxCardName}: Minimum credit limit cannot be more than maximum credit limit`,
      };
  }};
return errors;
};

const CreditCard: FunctionComponent<Props> = ({ context, viewMode }) => {
return (
<DimensionedCustomSection
sectionSetType={RATES_TYPE_CARD}
sectionName={'Rates'}
sectionSetFields={[...fields1, ...fields2]}
context={context}
viewMode={viewMode}
uploadValidator={uploadValidator}
tableFields={tableFields}
    [>
  };
};

export default CreditCard;

viewMode === ViewMode.View,
tenant.tenantId,
onImageUpload,
mediaUrl,
productId,
).createComponent()}
</GridContainer>
</CardBody>
</Card>
  };
}

// be treated as such
const fetchSecuredLoanProductOptions = (
context: any,
viewMode: string,
setInitialState: (initialState: any) => void,
setError: (initialState: any) => void,
setDefaultState: () => void,
): Promise<any> => {
if (viewMode === 'create') {
setDefaultState();
return Promise.resolve();
}
const { productId, tenantId, recordState } = utils.contextHelper(context);

  if (!productId)
throw new Error(
`context did not have productId: ${JSON.stringify(context)}`,
    };
if (!tenantId)
throw new Error(
`context did not have tenant data: ${JSON.stringify(context)}`,
    };

  const config: Config = { headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId } };
const url = `${process.env.REACT_APP_CORE_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL}product-options/${productId}`;
const fetch = client2(url, 'GET', config);

  return fetch
.then((data: any) => {
const sets: any[] = data.payload;
if (!Array.isArray(sets) || sets.length === 0) {
if (recordState === 'Draft') {
setDefaultState();
} else {
setError({
message: `No sets found for product ${productId}, Response: ${JSON.stringify(
data,
)}`,
          }];
}
}

      const initialState: any = {};
sets.forEach((set, i) => {
if (!set.specificationJSON)
throw new Error(
`Set ${i} did not have specificationJSON field.  ${JSON.stringify(
sets,
)}`,
          ];
initialState[i] = utils.formatServiceObject(
utils.tryParseJson(set.specificationJSON),
        };
      }};
setInitialState(initialState);
    }}
.catch((error) => {
console.log(error);
setError(error);



















  const url = `${process.env.REACT_APP_CORE_PRODUCTS_API_BASE_URL}product-options/${productId}`;
const tenantId =
coreProductContext?.tenant?.id ?? coreProductContext?.props?.tenant?.id;
const config = {
headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId } as any,
body: payload as any,
};

  // We retire old sets of Credit Card product-options and create new ones.
// We do not PUT with Credit Card product-options.
config.headers['X-B64-ENCODED'] = 'true';
config.headers['Content-Type'] = 'text/plain';
config.body = Base64.encode(JSON.stringify(payload));

  return client2(url, 'POST', config, false);
};

export default SecuredProductOptions;

interface Base {
  id: string;
  label: string:
  name?: string;
  required: boolean;
  disabled?: boolean;
  defaultValue: string I string[] I boolean;
  stateoverrideKey?: string:
  fieldValidation?: FieldValidation;
  column?: 1 | 2;
  component?: any; // ToDo
  style?: React.cssProperties;
  helpText?: string;
}

export interface FieldValidation {
  method: (...args: any[]) => boolean;
  validwhen: boolean;
  message: string;
  arg?: any;
}

export interface TextSecondaryField {
id: string;
label: string;
prefix?: string;
suffix?: string;
fieldValidation?: FieldValidation;
}

export interface TextField extends Base {
type: 'text';
prefix?: string;
suffix?: string;
secondaryField?: TextSecondaryField;
}

export interface IntegerField extends Omit<TextField, 'type'> {
type: 'integer';
}

export interface TextArea extends Base {
  type: 'textarea';
  multiline: boolean;
  rows: number;















































label: string;
defaultValue: boolean;
};
}

type CustomSectionField =
| TextField
| IntegerField
| TextArea
| TextAreaMarkdown
| CustomSectionRadio
| CustomSectionMultiSelect
| CustomSectionSelect
| CustomSectionCheckbox
| CustomSectionSwitch
| SwitchableInput;

export default CustomSectionField;

expect(r).toBeInTheDocument();

const _assertCheckbox = (
checked: boolean,
text: string,
displayIndex?: number,
) => {
let cb;
if (displayIndex !== undefined) {
displayIndex = displayIndex - 1;
const r = screen.getAllByLabelText(text) as HTMLInputElement[];
if (!r[displayIndex]) {
fail(`Could not find checkbox ${displayIndex} with text ${text}`);
}
cb = r[displayIndex];
} else {
cb = screen.getByLabelText(text) as HTMLInputElement;
}
checked ? expect(cb).toBeChecked() : expect(cb).not.toBeChecked();


const assertUnchecked = (text: string, index?: number) =>
_assertCheckbox(false, text, index);
const assertChecked = (text: string, index?: number) =>
_assertCheckbox(true, text, index);
const stateFromCache = (ctxState: any) => {
if (!ctxState) throw new Error('No ctxState provided');
if (!ctxState.cache) throw new Error('Could not find cache in ctxState');
return ctxState.cache;
};
const getCustomSetFromCache = (ctxState: any, setIndex: number) => {
const cache = stateFromCache(ctxState);
if (!cache[setIndex])
throw new Error(
`Index: ${setIndex} does not match a custom section set.  Cache: ${JSON.stringify(
ctxState.cache,
)} `,
    };
return cache[setIndex];
};

target: { value: text },
  }};
};

const getTableFieldByid = (
  dom: any.
  id: string,.
  setIndex: number,
  tableIndex: number,
} => {
  const htmlId = utils.htmlId(setIndex, id, tableIndex);
  const input = getById(dom.container, htmlId);
  if(!input){
    throw new Error('Cannot find input with id '${id}' and htmlId '${htmlId}'');
  }
  return input as HTMLInputElement;
};

const getTableCell = (
  dom: any.
  tableIndex: number,
  colIndex: number,
  colFieldKey: string,
  sectionSetIndex = 0,
} => {
  const htmlId = utils.htmlTableCellId(
    sectionSetIndex,
    colFieldKey.
tableIndex,
colIndex,
  };
const input = getById(dom.container, htmlId);
if (!input) {
throw new Error(
`Cannot find table cell with column key '${colFieldKey}' and htmlId '${htmlId}'`,
    };
}
return input as HTMLInputElement;
};

import { minMaxValueCheck, minMaxValidationMessage } from '../validations';

test('minMaxValueCheck validator with different inputs and formats', () => {
/** No max or min value */
expect(minMaxValueCheck('5', {})).toEqual(true);
  /＊＊＊/
expect(minMaxValueCheck('5', { maxValue: 4 })).toEqual(false);
expect(minMaxValueCheck('5', { maxValue: 5 })).toEqual(true);
expect(minMaxValueCheck('0', { minValue: 1 })).toEqual(false);
expect(minMaxValueCheck('1', { maxValue: 1 })).toEqual(true);
  /＊＊＊/
expect(minMaxValueCheck('$155.34', { maxValue: 4 })).toEqual(false);
expect(minMaxValueCheck('$155.34', { maxValue: 200 })).toEqual(true);
expect(minMaxValueCheck('0.00', { minValue: 1 })).toEqual(false);
expect(minMaxValueCheck('1.00%', { maxValue: 1 })).toEqual(true);
}};

import { CustomSectionState } from './types';

export interface SectionErrorCheck {
type: 'SECTION_ERROR_CHECK';
}

export interface SectionRemovedAction {
type: 'SECTION_REMOVED';
sectionIndex: number;
}

export interface StateResetAction {
type: 'STATE_RESET';
newState: CustomSectionState;
}

export interface SectionSetAdded {
type: 'SECTION_SET_ADDED';
}

export interface SetFieldValueChanged {
type: 'SET_FIELD_VALUE_CHANGED';
  sectionSetIndex: number;
  fieldId: string;
value: string;
}

export interface TableAdded {
type: 'TABLE_ADDED';
  sectionSetIndex: number;
}





















value: string;
}

export type CustomSectionAction =
| SectionRemovedAction
| StateResetAction
| SectionSetAdded
| SetFieldValueChanged
| TableAdded
| TableRemoved
| TableFieldValueChanged
| TableCellValueChanged
| SectionErrorCheck;

const CustomSectionstateMgmtCtx = React.createContext<StateMgmt I null>(null);

export const useCustomSectionstateMgmt = () => {
  const stateMgmt = useContext(CustomSectionstateMgmtCtx);
  if (!stateMgmt)
    throw new Error(
      'Attempting to use custom section state mgmt outside of a custom section',
    };
  return stateMgmt;
};

const CustomSectionViewmodeCtx = React.createContext<ViewMode>('view');
const CustomSectionSetFieldsCtx = React.createContext<CustomSectionField[]>([]);
const CustomSectionTableConfigCtx =
  React.createContext<SectionTableConfig<any> I null>(null);

export const useCustomSectionConfig = () => {
const setFields = useContext(CustomSectionSetFieldsCtx);
const tableConfig = useContext(CustomSectionTableConfigCtx);
const viewMode = useContext(CustomSectionViewmodeCtx);
return {
setFields,
tableConfig,
viewMode,
};
};

const CustomSectionDimensionSpecCtx =
React.createContext<DimensionSpecification | null>(null);
export const useCustomSectionDimensions = () => {
const dimensions = useContext(CustomSectionDimensionSpecCtx);
return dimensions;
};

const createListenterValFN = (
sectionName: CustomSectionProps['sectionName'],
customValidator: CustomSectionProps['uploadValidator'],
setFormErrorState: any,
buildCacheFN: () => void,
tablesRequired?: boolean,
) => {
return (context: any) => {
buildCacheFN();

    // fetch the latest state of the product, rates table, and errors from cache
const sectionSetList = utils.getCachedState(context);

    // pull the errors that were created from buildCacheFN from the cache. work around
// to allow the continued use of the context object
const cacheErrors = utils.getErrorCache(context);

    // This is what prevents the product from being uploaded if it has errors
const setCoreProductPageError = (msg: string) =>
context.updateError(sectionName, msg);

    let errors: string[] = [];
if (cacheErrors !== undefined) {
const { foundInvalidErrors, foundRequiredErrors } = cacheErrors;
if (foundInvalidErrors) {
errors.push('- Fix the highlighted errors.');
}






const tablesNotGenerated = sectionSetList.some(
(set) => Object.keys(set.tables).length === 0,
      };
      if (tablesNotGenerated){
        errors.push(
           - Tables for each set must be generated and filled out before submitting',
        };
      }
    }

if (customValidator) {
errors = errors.concat(customValidator(sectionSetList));
}

    setCoreProductPageError(errors.length > 0 ? 'Form errors' : -');
    setFormErrorState(errors);
  };
};

export interface UploadContext {
coreProductContext: any;
productId: string;
newVersionOfSubcomponents: boolean;
recordState: string;
tableConfig?: SectionTableConfig<any>;
dimensionSpec?: DimensionSpecification;
}

const createCustomUploadFunc = (
customUploadFunction: (
uploadContext: UploadContext,
sectionSets: any[],
) => void,
) => {
return (
context: any,
productId: string,
newVersionOfSubcomponents: boolean,
recordState: string,
) => {
const sets = context.getCachedObject(CACHE_OBJECT_KEY_RATES) as any[];
const tableConfig = context.getCachedObject(
CACHE_OBJECT_KEY_TABLE_CONFIG,
) as SectionTableConfig<any> | undefined;
const dimensionSpec = context.getCachedObject(
CACHE_OBJECT_KEY_DIMENSION_SPEC,
) as DimensionSpecification | undefined;

    const uploadContext: UploadContext = {
coreProductContext: context,
productId,
newVersionOfSubcomponents,
recordState,
tableConfig,
dimensionSpec,
};

    return customUploadFunction(uploadContext, sets);
};
};

startDate: formattedSet.startDate ?? '',
endDate: formattedSet.endDate,
};
utils.setUploadRecordState(setForUpload, recordState);
return setForUpload;
    }};

    const url = `${process.env.REACT_APP_RATES_API_BASE_URL}product-rates/${productId}`;
const config = {
headers: { 'X-TENANT-ID': tenantId } as any,
body: setsForUpload,
};
return client2(url, newVersionOfSubcomponents ? 'POST' : 'PUT', config);
};
};

const addSectionstateToCache = (context: any, state: CustomSectionstate) => {
  const result = Object.values(state);
  context.cacheObject(CACHE_OBJECT_KEY_RATES, result);
  return;
};

const addTableConfigToCache = (
  context: any,
  tableConfig?: SectionTableConfig<any>,
} => {
  context.cacheObject(CACHE_OBJECT_KEY_TABLE_CONFIG, tableConfig);
};

const addDimensionSpecToCache = (
  context: any,
  spec?: DimensionSpecification,
} => {
  context.cacheObject(CACHE_OBJECT_KEY_DIMENSION_SPEC, spec);
};

const CustomSection: React.FC<CustomSectionProps> = ({
  sectionSetType,
  sectionSetFields,
  context,
  uploadValidator,
  viewMode,
  sectionName,
  tableConfig,
  children,
customFetchFunction(
  customUploadFunction,
  customstateListener,
  dimensionSpecificationData,
  tablesRequired,
) =>
  const [loading, setLoading] = React.usestate(true);
  const [errorResponse, setErrorResponse] = React.usestate(null);

  const dimensionSpecification = dimensionSpecificationData?.result;

  const { state, stateMgmt } = useCustomSection(
    sectionSetFields,
context,
tableConfig,
dimensionSpecification,
customStateListener,
  };
const { stateDispatch } = stateMgmt;
const { formErrors } = state;

  addSectionStateToCache(context, state.sectionState);
addTableConfigToCache(context, tableConfig);
addDimensionSpecToCache(context, dimensionSpecification);

  useEffect(() => {
if (!tableConfig) return;
if (!dimensionSpecificationData) return;
const { delta, result: dimensionSpec } = dimensionSpecificationData;
if (delta) {
stateDispatch({
type: 'STATE_RESET',
newState: utils.rebuildTableStateFromDelta(
dimensionSpec,
delta,
          tableConfig.
          state.sectionstate,
        },
      }};
    else {
      stateDispatch(
        type: 'STATE_RESET',
        newState: {
          0: {
            ...utils.defaultStateFromFields(sectionSetFields),
            tables: utils.initTableStateFromDimensions(
              dimensionSpec,
              tableConfig,
            ],
          },
        },
      }};
    }
   ,[dimensionSpecificationData]);

  useEffect(() => {
    context.addValidationListener(
      createListenterValFN(
        sectionName,
uploadValidator,
stateMgmt.setFormErrors,
() => stateDispatch({ type: 'SECTION_ERROR_CHECK' }),
tablesRequired,
      },
    };

    context.addUploadListener(
customUploadFunction
? createCustomUploadFunc(customUploadFunction)
: createDefaultUploadFunc(sectionSetType),
    };
}, []);

  useEffect(() => {
if (!loading) return;
if (viewMode === 'view') {
if (customFetchFunction) {
customFetchFunction(
context,
viewMode,
(newState: CustomSectionState) =>
stateDispatch({ type: 'STATE_RESET', newState }),
          setErrorResponse,
          () => {,
).then(() => setLoading(false));
} else {
defaultFetchSetsRatesMgmt(
context,
(newState: CustomSectionState) =>
stateDispatch({ type: 'STATE_RESET', newState }),
setErrorResponse,
() => {},
).then(() => setLoading(false));
}
return;
}

    const setDefaultState = () => {
stateDispatch({
type: 'STATE_RESET',
        newState: {
          0: {
             ..utils.defaultStateFromFields(sectionSetFields),
           tables: {},
          },
        },
      }};
    };

    if (viewMode === 'create'){
      if (tableConfig && dimensionSpecification) {
        stateDispatch({
          type: 'STATE_RESET',
          newstate: {
           0:
               ..utils.defaultStateFromFields(sectionSetFields),
              tables: utils.initTableStateFromDimensions(
               dimensionSpecification,
               tableConfig,
              ],
            },
          },
        }];
       else if (customFetchFunction) {
        customFetchFunction(
          context,
          viewMode,
          (newState: CustomSectionState) =>
            stateDispatch({ type: 'STATE_RESET', newState }),
          setErrorResponse,
          setDefaultState,
        ).then(() => setLoading(false));
      else {
        setDefaultState();
      }
      setLoading(false);
    } else if (viewMode === 'edit'){
      if (customFetchFunction){
        customFetchFunction(
          context,
          viewMode,
          (newState: CustomSectionState) =>
            stateDispatch({ type: 'STATE_RESET', newState }),
          setErrorResponse,
          setDefaultState,
        ).then(() => setLoading(false));















































}
close={true}
color="danger"
closeNotification={() => stateMgmt.setFormErrors([])}
                  [>
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
            ]}
{children}
          >[>
</CardBody>
</Card>
    };
};

  return (
<CustomSectionDimensionSpecCtx.Provider
value={dimensionSpecification ?? null}
>
<CustomSectionSetFieldsCtx.Provider value={sectionSetFields}>
<CustomSectionStateMgmtCtx.Provider value={stateMgmt}>
<CustomSectionTableConfigCtx.Provider value={tableConfig ?? null}>
<CustomSectionViewmodeCtx.Provider value={viewMode}>
<CustomSectionStateCtx.Provider value={state}>
{renderCard()}
</CustomSectionStateCtx.Provider>
</CustomSectionViewmodeCtx.Provider>
</CustomSectionTableConfigCtx.Provider>
</CustomSectionStateMgmtCtx.Provider>
</CustomSectionSetFieldsCtx.Provider>
</CustomSectionDimensionSpecCtx.Provider>
  };
};

export default CustomSection;

      </GridItem>
    };
  };

  return(

      {renderAddSectionSetBtn())
    >[>
  };
};

notApplicable,
colFieldKey,
colIndex,
table,

.filter((row) => VALID_NOT_APPLICABLE_CELL_TYPES.includes(row.cellType))

},

customChangeHandler: (value, colFieldKey, colIndex, table) => {

table.forEach((col, i) => {
col[colFieldKey] = i === colIndex; // Set selected checkbox to true, all others to false
      }};
},
defaultValue: () => false,
},
percentage: {
render: (props) => <TableCellInput {...props} postDisplaySymbol={'%'} />,
defaultValue: () => '',
isValid: decimalUtils.checkIsValidFloat,
onUpload: (v) => parseFloat(v),
onLoad: (v) => v.toString(),
},
number: {
render: (props) => <TableCellInput {...props} />,
isValid: (value) => !Number.isNaN(value),
defaultValue: () => '',
onUpload: (v) => parseFloat(v),
onLoad: (v) => v.toString(),
  },
  percentageOrNA: {
    render:(props)=>(
      <TableCellInput
        {...props?
        postDisplaySymbol={'%'}
        formatUnsetValues={true}
      />
    },
    defaultValue:()=> ',
    isValid:(value, column) => {
      const unsetValue: boolean =
        !value |l
(typeof value === 'string' &&
regexUtils.isNotApplicableString(value as string));

      if (unsetValue) {
return true;
}

      return decimalUtils.checkIsValidFloat(value) || value === NOT_APPLICABLE;
},
onUpload: (v) => {
const unsetValue: boolean =
!v ||
(typeof v === 'string' &&
regexUtils.isNotApplicableString(v as string));
if (unsetValue) {
return 'N/A';
}
return parseFloat(v);
},
onLoad: (v) => v.toString(),
},
};

import * as utils from './utils';
import  get } from 'lodash';

const usestyles = makestyles(styles);

interface Props {
  htmlId: string;
  field: CustomSectionField;
  sectionSetIndex: number;
  tableIndex?: number;
value: any;
secondaryValue?: any;
renderCustomWrapper?: ({
children,
}: {
children: React.ReactNode;
}) => JSX.Element;
}

const formatValue = (
field: CustomSectionField,
value: any,
secondaryValue?: boolean,
): string => {
const prefix =
field.type === 'text' && secondaryValue
? get(field, 'secondaryField.prefix', '')
: get(field, 'prefix', '');
const suffix =
field.type === 'text' && secondaryValue
? get(field, 'secondaryField.suffix', '')
: get(field, 'suffix', '');
let formattedValue = value as string;

  if (field.type === 'multiselect') {
const valuesArray: string[] = Array.isArray(value)
? value
: value.split(',');
const trimmedValues = valuesArray.map((value) => {
const trimmed = value.trim();
const option = field.options.find(
(opt) =>
opt.hasOwnProperty('value') &&
(opt as CustomSectionRadioOption).value === trimmed,
      };
return option === undefined
? trimmed
: (option as CustomSectionRadioOption).label;
    }};
formattedValue = trimmedValues.join(', ');
} else if (Array.isArray(value)) {
// if the product option field value is an array, trim whitespace
// and join using commas
const valuesArray = value as string[];
const trimmedValues = valuesArray.map((value) => value.trim());

} else {
if (value === 'false' || value === false) formattedValue = 'No';
if (value === 'true' || value === true) formattedValue = 'Yes';
// add a prefix if one is defined by product option fields
formattedValue = `${prefix ? `${prefix} ` : ''}${formattedValue}${
suffix ? ` ${suffix}` : ''
}`;
}
return formattedValue;
};

const CustomSectionViewField = ({
htmlId,
field,
sectionSetIndex,
value: incomingValue,
tableIndex,
secondaryValue: incomingSecondaryValue,
renderCustomWrapper,
}: Props) => {
const value = incomingValue ?? '';
const secondaryValue = incomingSecondaryValue ?? '';
const classes = useStyles();
const classes = useStyles();
  const renderSecondaryField = () => {
const formattedValue = formatValue(field, secondaryValue, true);
if (field.type === 'multiselect' && field.customFieldId) {
const htmlId = utils.htmlId(
sectionSetIndex,
field.customFieldId,
tableIndex,
      };
return (
<ViewSetting
label={`Custom ${field.label}`}
settingName={htmlId}
settingValue={formattedValue}
classes={classes}
        [>
      };
} else if (field.type === 'text' && field.secondaryField) {
const htmlId = utils.htmlId(
sectionSetIndex,
field.secondaryField.id,
tableIndex,
      };
      return(
        <ViewSetting
          label={field.secondaryField.label}
          settingName={htmlId}
          settingValue={formattedValue}
          classes={classes}
        [>
      };
    } else if (field.type === 'switchableInput'){
      return(
        <ViewSetting
          label={field.switchField.label}
          settingName={htmlId}
          settingValue={formattedValue}
          classes={classes}
        [>
      };
    else
      return null;
    }
  };

  const WrapperComponent = renderCustomwrapper
    ? renderCustomWrapper
    : ({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode ): JSX.Element => (
        <GridContainer style={{ width: 'calc(100% + 80px)' }}>
          <GridItem xs={6}>{children}</GridItem>
        </GridContainer>
      };

  return(
    <WrapperComponent>
      >>
        <ViewSetting
          label={field.label}
          helpText={field.helpText}
settingName={htmlId}
settingValue={formatValue(field, value)}
classes={classes}
        [>
{renderSecondaryField()}
      >[>
</WrapperComponent>
  };
};

export default CustomSectionViewField;

import CustomSectionField, {
CustomSectionMultiSelect,
SwitchableInput,
TextField,
} from './types/CustomSectionField';
import * as A from './actions';
import {
GenericSectionSetState,
SectionSetState,
SectionTableState,
} from './types';
import customSectionTableCellMap, {
TableCellType,
} from './CustomSectionTableCell';
import { setErrorMsg, deleteErrorMsg, extractErrorMsg } from './utils';
import { IS_DEFAULT_COLUMN_FIELD_KEY } from './dimensions/ProductTableColumnDictionary';

const unpackCustomValues = (valuesStr: string) => {
if (valuesStr === '') return [];
return valuesStr.split(',').map((s) => s.trim());
};

const tryHandleMultiSelect = (
value: any,
fieldId: string,
fields: CustomSectionField[],
draft: any,
checkRequired = false,
): boolean => {
const multiSelectThatChanged = fields.find(
(f) => f.id === fieldId && f.type === 'multiselect',
  };
const customFieldThatChanged = fields.find(
(f) => (f as CustomSectionMultiSelect).customFieldId === fieldId,
  };

  if (multiSelectThatChanged) {
const field = multiSelectThatChanged as CustomSectionMultiSelect;
let values = value as string[];
if (field.customFieldId) {
const valuesStr = draft[field.customFieldId];
if (valuesStr === undefined)
throw new Error(
`Custom field value was not found in state draft with customFieldId: ${field.customFieldId}`,
        };
values = values.concat(unpackCustomValues(draft[field.customFieldId]));
}
if (field.required && values.length === 0) {
setErrorMsg(draft, field.id, 'Required');
} else {
deleteErrorMsg(draft, field.id);
}
return true;
} else if (customFieldThatChanged) {
const field = customFieldThatChanged as CustomSectionMultiSelect;
const values = unpackCustomValues(value);
values.concat(draft[field.id]);
if (field.required && values.length === 0) {
setErrorMsg(draft, field.id, 'Required');
} else {
deleteErrorMsg(draft, field.id);
}
return true;
}
return false;
};

const tryHandleSecondaryField = (
value: string,
fieldId: string,
fields: CustomSectionField[],
draft: any,

const firstFieldThatChanged = fields.find(
(f) => f.id === fieldId && f.type === 'text' && f.secondaryField,
  };
const secondFieldThatChanged = fields.find(
(f) => (f as TextField).secondaryField?.id === fieldId,
  };

  const _field = firstFieldThatChanged
? firstFieldThatChanged
: secondFieldThatChanged;
if (!_field) return false;
const field = _field as TextField;
const secondaryField = field.secondaryField;
if (!secondaryField) throw new Error('To appease the compiler');
// After this line we are working with a Text field with a secondary field configured, even
// though the compiler is not picking that up

  const firstId = field.id;
const secId = secondaryField.id;

  if (field.required && draft[firstId] === '' && draft[secId] === '') {
setErrorMsg(draft, firstId, 'Required');
setErrorMsg(draft, secId, 'Required');
return true;
} else {
deleteErrorMsg(draft, firstId);
deleteErrorMsg(draft, secId);
}

  let handled = false;
if (draft[firstId] !== '' && field.fieldValidation) {
handled = true;
const toCheck = firstFieldThatChanged ? value : draft[firstId];
const { validWhen, method, message, arg } = field.fieldValidation;
if (method(toCheck, arg) === validWhen) {
deleteErrorMsg(draft, firstId);
} else {
setErrorMsg(draft, firstId, message);
}
}

  if (draft[secId] !== '' && secondaryField.fieldValidation) {
handled = true;
const toCheck = secondFieldThatChanged ? value : draft[secId];
const { validWhen, method, message, arg } = secondaryField.fieldValidation;

deleteErrorMsg(draft, secId);
} else {
setErrorMsg(draft, secId, message);
}
}
return handled;
};

const tryHandleSwitchableField = (
value: string,
fieldId: string,
fields: CustomSectionField[],
draft: any,
): boolean => {
const inputThatChanged = fields.find(
(f) => f.id === fieldId && f.type === 'switchableInput',
  };
const switchThatChanged = fields.find(
(f) => (f as SwitchableInput).switchField?.id === fieldId,
  };
const field = (inputThatChanged ? inputThatChanged : switchThatChanged) as
| SwitchableInput
| undefined;

  if (!field) return false;

  const isOn = draft[field.switchField.id];
if (isOn && draft[field.id] === '') {
setErrorMsg(draft, field.id, 'Required');
} else if (isOn && field.fieldValidation) {
const { validWhen, method, message, arg } = field.fieldValidation;
if (method(value, arg) === validWhen) {
deleteErrorMsg(draft, field.id);
} else {
setErrorMsg(draft, field.id, message);
}
} else {
deleteErrorMsg(draft, field.id);
}

  return true;
};

export const indexOfGapDecimal = (
objs: Array<{ start?: number | string; end?: number | string }>,
increment: number,
): number | undefined => {
let i = 1;
for (const second of objs.slice(1)) {
const first = objs[i - 1];
if (!first.end) return;
if (!second.start) return;
const start = parseFloat(second.start.toString());
const end = parseFloat(first.end.toString());
    const actualIncrement = (start - end).toFixed(2);
    if (actualIncrement !== increment.toFixed(2).tostring()) {
      return i - 1;
    }
    i=i+1;
  }
  return;
};

export const indexofGapNumber = (
  objs: Array<{ start?: number I string; end?: number  string }>,
  increment: number,
): number | undefined => {
  if (objs.length === 0 l objs.length === 1) return;
  const parsedobjs = objs.map((obj) => {
    return {
      start:
        typeof obj.start === 'string'
          ? parseInt(obj.start, 10)
          : obj.start ?? 0,











































  if ((currentUnixTime > endDate) {
    set.recordstate = 'Expired';
    return;
  }

  set.recordstate = 'Active';
}

    calculateRecordstate(setForUpload, currentTime);
  f else{

export const formatServiceObject = (valuesOrSpecJSON: OBJ) => {
if (valuesOrSpecJSON.hasOwnProperty('tables')) {
if (!Array.isArray(valuesOrSpecJSON.tables)) {
valuesOrSpecJSON.tables = [];
}
valuesOrSpecJSON.tables = listToIndexedObj(valuesOrSpecJSON.tables);
} else {
valuesOrSpecJSON.tables = {};
}
return valuesOrSpecJSON as SectionSetState;
};

export const formatServiceObjectForUpload = (valuesOrSpecJSON: OBJ) => {
// Need to do this to prevent conflicts with immerjs package
valuesOrSpecJSON = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(valuesOrSpecJSON));
if (
valuesOrSpecJSON.hasOwnProperty('tables') &&
typeof valuesOrSpecJSON.tables === 'object'
  } {
valuesOrSpecJSON.tables = Object.values(valuesOrSpecJSON.tables);
}
if (!valuesOrSpecJSON.tables) {
valuesOrSpecJSON.tables = [];
}
return valuesOrSpecJSON as SectionSetState;
};

componentReference: 'IMAGE-UPLOADER',
}};

import styles from 'assets/jss/material-dashboard-pro-react/views/regularFormsStyle';
import CustomFormLabel from 'components/CustomFormLabel/CustomFormLabel';
import CustomInput from 'components/CustomInput/CustomInput';
import GridContainer from 'components/Grid/GridContainer';
import GridItem from 'components/Grid/GridItem';
import React from 'react';
import { CustomFieldComponentParams } from '../types/FieldTypes';
import { Rates } from '../types/RateTypes';

const useStyles = makeStyles(styles);

export const DiscountedAprSwitch: CustomFieldComponentParams = (
ratesDisplayOrder: number,
rates: Rates,
fieldErrorState: { [key: string]: { isInvalid: boolean; message: string } },
index: number,
updateRatesMap: (
ratesId: string,
ratesFunc: (prevRates: Rates) => Rates,
) => void | null,

displayOrder: number,
entries: [string, string | boolean][],
) => void,
validateChange: (
event: any,
ratesDisplayOrder: number,
optionalMultiValues: string[] | null,
tableId?: number | undefined,
) => Promise<null | undefined>,
tableId?: number,
) => {
const classes = useStyles();
return (
<GridContainer key={index}>
<GridItem xs={12} lg={10}>
<FormControlLabel
className={classes.labelHorizontal}
control={
<Switch
id={`${ratesDisplayOrder}-discountedApr-input`}
checked={rates.valuesJSON && rates.valuesJSON.discountedApr}
onChange={(event) => {
updateRatesMap(rates.id, (prevRates: Rates) => {

...prevRates,
valuesJSON: prevRates.valuesJSON && {
...prevRates.valuesJSON,
discountedApr:
rates.valuesJSON && !rates.valuesJSON.discountedApr,
},
};
               }];
// Doing this to clear any discountDescription validation that
// may have been appearing before discountedApr checkbox value
// is toggled
const mockDiscountDescEvent = {
target: {
id: `${ratesDisplayOrder}-discountDescription-input`,
value: '',
},
};
validateChange(
mockDiscountDescEvent,
ratesDisplayOrder,
null,
tableId,
                ];
}}
value={rates.valuesJSON && rates.valuesJSON.discountedApr}
classes={{
switchBase: classes.switchBase,
checked: classes.switchChecked,
thumb: classes.switchIcon,
track: classes.switchBar,
}}
            [>
}
classes={{
label: classes.label,
}}
label="Discounted APR"
style={{
marginRight: '100px',
}}
        [>
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
  };
};



export const DiscountedAprDescInput: CustomFieldComponentParams = (
ratesDisplayOrder: number,
rates: Rates,
fieldErrorState: { [key: string]: { isInvalid: boolean; message: string } },
index: number,
updateRatesMap: (
ratesId: string,
ratesFunc: (prevRates: Rates) => Rates,
) => void | null,
updateTableFields: (
displayOrder: number,
entries: [string, string | boolean][],
) => void,
validateChange: (
event: any,
ratesDisplayOrder: number,
optionalMultiValues: string[] | null,
tableId?: number | undefined,
) => Promise<null | undefined>,
tableId?: number,
) => {
const classes = useStyles();
return (
<GridContainer key={index}>
<GridItem xs={6} lg={6}>
<CustomFormLabel
className={classes.labelHorizontal}
styleType="textFieldFactory"
label="Discount Description"
required={rates.valuesJSON && rates.valuesJSON.discountedApr}
        [>
</GridItem>
<GridItem xs={6} lg={6}>
<CustomInput
id={`${ratesDisplayOrder}-discountDescription-input`}
formControlProps={{
fullWidth: true,
style: {
paddingTop: '0px',
paddingBottom: '0px',
},
}}
error={
fieldErrorState[`${ratesDisplayOrder}-discountDescription-input`] &&

.isInvalid
}
inputProps={{
value:
rates.valuesJSON &&
rates.valuesJSON.discountDescription &&
rates.valuesJSON.discountDescription,
type: 'textarea',
multiline: true,
rows: 4,
style: { border: '1px solid #495057', width: '275px' },
disabled: rates.valuesJSON && !rates.valuesJSON.discountedApr,
onChange: (event) => {
const value = event.target.value;
updateRatesMap(rates.id, (prevRates: Rates) => {
return {
...prevRates,
valuesJSON: prevRates.valuesJSON && {
...prevRates.valuesJSON,
discountDescription: value,
},
};
});
validateChange(event, ratesDisplayOrder, null);
},
}}
helperText={
fieldErrorState[`${ratesDisplayOrder}-discountDescription-input`] &&
fieldErrorState[`${ratesDisplayOrder}-discountDescription-input`]
.message
}
        [>
</GridItem>
</GridContainer>
  };
};

DiscountedAprDescInput.displayName = 'DiscountedAprDescInput';

import { DateTime } from 'luxon';
import { v4 as uuid } from 'uuid';
import {
RATES_TYPE_NON_AUTO,
RATES_TYPE_AUTO,
RATES_TYPE_CARD,
RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_LINE_OF_CREDIT,
RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_LOANS,
RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_UNSECURED,
} from '../../constants/RateTypes';
import { Rates } from '../types/RateTypes';

export const DEFAULT_MIN_RATE = 1000;
export const DEFAULT_MAX_RATE = 80000;

export function createDefaultRates(
ratesType: string,
minMaxCreditLimit: { min: number; max: number },
): Map<string, Rates> {
const ratesId = uuid();

  let defaultRates;

  switch (ratesType) {
case RATES_TYPE_AUTO:
defaultRates = DEFAULT_AUTO_RATES;
break;
case RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_LOANS:
defaultRates = DEFAULT_PERSONAL_LOANS_RATES;
break;
case RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_LINE_OF_CREDIT:
if (!minMaxCreditLimit) {
minMaxCreditLimit = { min: DEFAULT_MIN_RATE, max: DEFAULT_MAX_RATE };
}
defaultRates = getDefaultPLOCRates(
minMaxCreditLimit.min,
minMaxCreditLimit.max,
      };
      break;
    case RATES_TYPE_CARD:
      defaultRates = getDefaultCCRates();
      break;
case RATES_TYPE_NON_AUTO:
defaultRates = DEFAULT_NON_AUTO_RATES;
break;
case RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_UNSECURED:
defaultRates = DEFAULT_PERSONAL_UNSECURED;
break;
default:
defaultRates = DEFAULT_AUTO_RATES;
break;
}

  const rates: Rates = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(defaultRates));
rates.id = ratesId;

  return new Map([[ratesId, rates]]);
}

export const getMinMaxCreditLimit = (context: any) => {
const minStr =
context.getSettingValue('MIN-CREDIT-LIMIT') || DEFAULT_MIN_RATE;
const maxStr =
context.getSettingValue('MAX-CREDIT-LIMIT') || DEFAULT_MAX_RATE;
return {
minStr,
maxStr,
min: parseInt(minStr, 10),
max: parseInt(maxStr, 10),
};
};

import { decimalUtils } from 'components/CoreProduct/util/DecimalUtils';
import { Term } from '../types/RateTypes';

export const indexOfCreditRangeGap = (terms: Term[]) => {
return indexOfGap(terms, 0.01);
};

export const indexOfGap = (
objs: Array<{ low?: number | string; high?: number | string }>,
increment: number,
): number | undefined => {
let i = 1;
for (const second of objs.slice(1)) {
const first = objs[i - 1];
if (!first.high) return;
if (!second.low) return;
if (
(decimalUtils.toDec(second.low) - decimalUtils.toDec(first.high)).toFixed(
        ],
) !== increment.toFixed(2).toString()
    } {
return i - 1;
}
i = i + 1;
}
return;
};

const props = getProps(RATES_TYPE_PERSONAL_LINE_OF_CREDIT, rates);
const dom = renderWithMockContext(<CreateRateSet {...props} />);
const helpers = useHelpers(dom);

    helpers.clickAddBalanceRange();
helpers.changeMinInput('100000000.00');
helpers.clickSubmit();
const r = dom.queryByText(
'Balance must be within core product minimum and maximum',
    };
expect(r).not.toBe(null);
  }};
}};

const getMockAutoRateSet = (overrides = {}, rateTableOverrides = []) => {
const rates = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_AUTO_RATES));
rates.valuesJSON.rateTables = rates.valuesJSON.rateTables.map((rt, i) => {
return {
...rates.valuesJSON.rateTables[i],
...(rateTableOverrides[i] ?? {}),
};
  }};
return {
...rates,
...overrides,
};
};

const getMockCCRateSet = (overrides = {}) => {
return {
...getDefaultCCRates(),
...overrides,
};
};

const deleteDefaultField = (rateSet) => {
rateSet.valuesJSON?.rateTables[0]?.terms?.forEach((term) => {
delete term.isDefault;
  }};
};

const getById = queryByAttribute.bind(null, 'id');

switch (k) {

return '1000000';
case 'MIN-CREDIT-LIMIT':
return '0';
default:

}

  fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: rates[o] } );

fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: rates[3] } });

  input = getById(container, `${idPrefix}-creditRangeRate4-input`);
fireEvent.change(input, { target: { value: rates[4] } });
}

function checkSingleRateSet(ratesMap, checkFunc) {
const ratesAsArray = ratesMap.values
? JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(Array.from(ratesMap.values())))
: [ratesMap];
checkFunc(ratesAsArray[0]);
}

import { makeStyles } from '@material-ui/core/styles';
import styles from 'assets/jss/material-dashboard-pro-react/views/regularFormsStyle';
import { DynamicInputField, SimpleAccordion } from 'commonui';
import Card from 'components/Card/Card';
import CardBody from 'components/Card/CardBody';
import CardHeader from 'components/Card/CardHeader';
import CardText from 'components/Card/CardText';
import { IEligibilityDetailsComponent } from 'components/Membership/types';
import React from 'react';
import { isKeyLink } from './useEligibility';
import { SCCategory, SCDetail, SCTitle } from './styles';

const useStyles = makeStyles(styles);

const EligiblityDetails = ({
model,
setDetails,
}: IEligibilityDetailsComponent): JSX.Element => {
const classes = useStyles();

  return (
<Card>
<CardHeader color="cuna1" text>
<CardText color="cuna1">
<h4
id="eligibility-identification-card-header"
className={classes.cardTitle}
>
Eligibility Details
</h4>
</CardText>
</CardHeader>
<CardBody>
{model.map(({ description, key, value, error }) => {
const isLink = isKeyLink(key);
return (
<SCCategory key={key}>
<SimpleAccordion
expandedOverride={true}
id={key}
title={key}
content={
<SCDetail key={key}>
<DynamicInputField
validateAllFields
dataField={{
fieldType: 'text',
fieldCode: `elegibility-description-${key}`,
fieldLabel: 'Description',
}}
value={description}
onChange={(_, newDescription) => {
setDetails(key, { description: newDescription });
}}
                    [>
<DynamicInputField
error={error}
validateAllFields
dataField={{
fieldType: 'text',
fieldCode: `elegibility-value-${key}`,
fieldLabel: 'Value',
placeHolder: isLink ? 'www.google.com' : '',
exposition: isLink
? [
{
content:
'Please provide a url. The http(s) protocol will be removed on save',
order: 1,
},
                             ]
                           ; [],
                      }}
122value={value}
                      onChange={(_, newValue)=> {
124setDetails(key, { value: newValue );
                      }}
                    [>
127/SCDetail>
                }
                buttonComponent={
130<sCTitle expandedText={key} collapsedText={key) />
                }
              />
133</ScCategory>
          };
        }]}
136</CardBody>
137 </Card>
  ];
};

export default EligiblityDetails;
